## 🧭 Reflow Risk (WCAG 1.4.10)

Page-level indicator: resizes to 320 CSS pixels wide and checks for horizontal overflow. This is a risk indicator, not a conformance verdict — see [Behavioral Accessibility Automation](https://github.com/mgifford/ACCESSIBILITY.md/blob/main/examples/BEHAVIORAL_ACCESSIBILITY_AUTOMATION.md) for what it can and cannot detect.

| Rule | Pages Affected | Documentation |

| [SIA-R111](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r111): Interactive elements have a sufficient target size | **69** of 100 | [View Rule](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r111) |
| [SIA-R73](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r73): Paragraphs of text have a line height that is not too small | **68** of 100 | [View Rule](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r73) |
| [SIA-R87](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r87): The first focusable element on the page is a skip link to the main content | **67** of 100 | [View Rule](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r87) |
| [SIA-R66](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r66): Text has enhanced contrast with its background | **66** of 100 | [View Rule](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r66) |
| [SIA-R57](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r57): Visible text content on the page is contained within a landmark region | **65** of 100 | [View Rule](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r57) |
| [SIA-R43](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r43) | **63** of 100 | [View Rule](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r43) |
| [SIA-R9](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r9) | **26** of 100 | [View Rule](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r9) |
| [SIA-R96](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r96) | **26** of 100 | [View Rule](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r96) |
| [SIA-R56](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r56): Landmarks with the same role are distinguishable by their accessible name | **10** of 100 | [View Rule](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r56) |
| [SIA-R68](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r68): Elements with an explicit ARIA role have the required owned/child elements | **4** of 100 | [View Rule](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r68) |

## 🔍 Cross-Page Patterns: Common HTML Issues

These HTML patterns cause the same accessibility errors across multiple pages. **Fix the pattern once in your codebase to fix it everywhere!**

### 🎯 Top Patterns to Fix (Highest Impact)

#### Pattern 1: Affects 64 page(s) - 64 occurrence(s)

**Scanner**: ALFA
**Rule**: [SIA-R111: Interactive elements have a sufficient target size](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r111)
**Issue**: Target has insufficient size

**HTML Pattern**:
```html
<button class="ccc-notify-button ccc-link ccc-tabbable ccc-notify-link">...</button>
```

**XPath** (use in browser DevTools):
```
/button[@class="ccc-notify-button ccc-link ccc-tabbable ccc-notify-link"]
```

**How to Replicate**:
1. Open any affected page in your browser
2. Press F12 to open DevTools
3. Go to Console tab
4. Run: `$x('/button[@class="ccc-notify-button ccc-link ccc-tabbable ccc-notify-link"]')`
5. The element will be highlighted

#### Rule: [SIA-R73: Paragraphs of text have a line height that is not too small](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r73)
**WCAG**: [SC 1.4.8](https://www.w3.org/TR/WCAG22/) (Level AAA)

**Failure 1:**
- First identified: 2026-08-22
- Message: The line height of the paragraph is `normal` which will result in a line height of less than 1.5
- HTML: `<p>...</p>`
- XPath: `/p`

**Failure 2:**
- First identified: 2026-08-22
- Message: The line height of the paragraph is `normal` which will result in a line height of less than 1.5
- HTML: `<p class="text-sm text-muted pl-2.5 pt-2 font-medium"> © Copyright 2026 Greater London Authority</p>`
- XPath: `/p[@class="text-sm text-muted pl-2.5 pt-2 font-medium"]`

### https://london.gov.uk/

#### Rule: [region](https://dequeuniversity.com/rules/axe/4.12/region?application=playwright)
